## 2026-10-06 R4-1b research UI and local hybrid retrieval (Codex)

- Baseline `aa1a3fe`; branch `codex/r4-1b-research-ui-20261006`. Restored
  database/project/personal/external routes, source table/details, item cards
  and metadata/revision workflows using legacy CSS and Preact. Four research
  tabs only; memory/inbox/brief and previews/attachments stay in later tasks.
  New strings have English entries. Kept the legacy structured JSON form.
- Source PATCH/reparse/open and filtering/sorting/pagination now operate on
  R4-1a records. Imports have no single-file cap. Migration 004 stores vectors
  in the same SQLite. A shared semantic service serves HTTP and AI tools;
  explicit setting/download actions, hashes, hardware check, worker inference,
  background warmup, dialogue index pauses, version checks and whole-request
  300ms keyword fallback retained. Progress is pushed, never polled each second.
- Minimal extra wiring: app.js owns service start/close; tools.js receives it;
  server.js exempts source imports from its generic body bound; static.js
  supplies installed KaTeX/fonts; database.js permits sqlite-vec loading;
  settings resource consumes the new default. Functional integration tests
  cover API/tool sharing and source management; no old-line/native code edits.
- Discovered → cause → repair → verification: async settings initialization
  overwrote download state with model-missing; initialize now respects the
  active download flag, tested with concurrent settings and pushed states.
  Warmup initially blocked startup; it now runs in background, tested with
  a held warmup. Editing populated textareas failed: implicit label included
  default body text; explicit aria-label fixes it, the same create/edit/replace
  browser path passes. Initial full check 67/68 retained in the local failure log.
- Windows real acceptance: clicked download in Settings, all 5 pinned files
  (135,392,183 bytes) pass SHA256; hardware AVX2/32GB, index 9/9, restart ready,
  Chinese semantic query returns hybrid and reads the matching full passage.
  Reference/new screenshots compared; restored database before workbench.
  Task-required top scope tabs/excluded future controls differ from old final
  view. Semantic controls are on the task-specified runtime page, old P33 on
  database settings. Original/new shell differences remain from R3/L1.
- Local evidence/screenshots: `.sciencescope-local/temp/r4-1b/`. Automated
  vector tests use injected fake embeddings, no model downloads. Paid model
  requests/tokens: 0/0; real local inference is reported separately.
- Final Windows `npm run check`: formatting/lint/types and 68/68 tests pass,
  zero skips (82.482s), including all 9 new research Edge paths, existing 3
  Edge paths and real DSH/local-model tests. No native code changed.

## 2026-10-06 R4-1a research base core (Codex)

- Baseline `ac23f0d`; branch `codex/r4-1a-research-core-20261006`. Rewrote
  research parsing/storage/retrieval under `app/services/research/`: PDF,
  DOCX, Markdown, LaTeX and text, unchanged 1200/2400 retrieval units with
  no document truncation. Originals live in `library/originals/` under the
  new data root. No old-line, UI, semantic retrieval or consolidation edits.
- Migration 003 adds sources, chunks, items, immutable revisions and FTS.
  v2 upgrade test proves backup and preservation of projects/messages.
  Superseded items retain their row, validity/provenance and search index;
  default queries select active records. Private items are excluded from AI
  search/read candidates. Each import/item mutation records a transactional
  `research.changed` event for the next UI stage.
- Added research HTTP routes and shared `research_search`/`research_read`
  tools, paged with total/remaining and full passage text. Citation locators
  preserve old composite, Chinese and fullwidth formats; unknown labels keep
  their text and reason. Source ids disambiguate project/personal numbering.
- Discovered → cause → repair → verification: Mammoth discarded empty HTML
  spans for DOCX page breaks, so explicit pages were missing. Its public
  document transform now retains nonempty styled page markers; genuine DOCX
  import/search/read proves pages 1/2 and equations. Without reliable breaks
  page remains null. Scans retain originals and report failed extraction; no OCR.
- Minimal extra integration file: `app/core/index.js` exposes the already
  owned shared DB connection so the service receives DB/events together;
  migration/API/scripted-meeting tests exercise that dependency end to end.
- Final Windows `npm run check`: 50/50, zero skipped; formatting, lint and
  types pass, including 8 new research tests, 3 Edge tests and existing real
  DSH/local-model paths. Paid model requests/tokens: 0/0. No UI change or new
  screenshot required. Final log: `.sciencescope-local/temp/r4-1a/check-final.log`.

## 2026-10-06 L1 Windows native shell (Codex)

- Baseline `f526cd4`; branch `codex/l1-windows-20261006`. Opt-in
  `SCIENCESCOPE_APP=new` starts `app/main.js --data` and loads its first JSON
  line's `ui`. Legacy startup remains the default. Development data defaults
  to `.sciencescope-local/data`; logs/WebView cache follow the new data root.
- `src-tauri/src/new_app.rs` holds launch resolution/readiness; the existing
  Job cleanup/window/dialog capabilities are reused. Added opener plugin and
  scoped HTTP(S) opening; home site buttons invoke it in native windows.
  Node publishes a cooperative `/api/shutdown` capability so SQLite closes
  before the Job fallback. Regression covers Chinese paths/CRLF readiness,
  legacy default and native shutdown/WAL cleanup.
- Discovered → cause → repair → verification: Explorer calls silently failed
  with `ENOENT` because the launcher PATH omitted Windows. `system.js` uses
  `SystemRoot/explorer.exe`, awaits spawn and reports failure; the project
  route awaits it. A regression and real native reopen prove Chinese folder
  opening and default-program HTML opening. No UI structure/style changes.
- Default test concurrency ran 16 files together; two cold DSH profiles each
  materialize about 419 dependency junctions on the external H drive. Startup
  timed out at 30 seconds (initial 38/40, then 40/41), while isolated runtime
  tests and full concurrency-2 comparison passed. `check`/`test:app` now use
  concurrency 2; production timeout unchanged. Final `npm run check` 42/42
  (0 skips), including three Edge tests/screenshots; Rust tests 2/2 and build
  passed. All failed logs remain local.
- Native real-model flow: provider/key/default configured in Settings,
  Chinese project/chat → streamed answer → main edits plans (agent_main
  revision) → workbench/change log → undo → Ctrl+K → English. Five HTTP200
  requests to `deepseek-v4.1-flash`, input 83,558 / output 1,264 / total 84,822
  tokens, no retry/failure; a secret-free counting gateway forwards DSH auth
  and session headers via the local network proxy. Key exists only in ignored
  credential files, never in environment, screenshots or request ledgers.
- WebView2 imports/fonts, minimize/maximize/close, real OS topbar drag,
  native Chinese folder selection/binding and Chinese ZIP download passed.
  Real IME trusted composing key/input events and Enter produced no message;
  candidate-to-Chinese commit completion was not fully verified (untrusted
  compositionend remains distinguished). Synthetic composing-Enter also passed.
- Normal native close/reopen exit 0 and clean WAL/SHM; a separate real-DSH/local
  model run captures host + active DSH before close and proves all owned PIDs
  gone afterward. That fixture adds one local model request, zero paid requests.
- Native/old-reference screenshots reviewed: no platform font/module failure;
  minor sidebar boundary/width and empty project chat wording/badge differences
  remain for Claude. English history retains participant names in Chinese.
  Stable search screenshot centers correctly; initial offset was animation.
  Evidence and long audit only in `.sciencescope-local/temp/l1-windows/`.
  Frozen `desktop/`, `scripts/`, `adapters/` unchanged; no R4/UI redesign.

## 2026-10-05 R2 Windows acceptance (Codex)

- Baseline `2afa40f`, branch `codex/r2-windows-20261005`, H-drive workspace.
  `npm install` succeeded (0 vulnerabilities); final `npm run check` passed

  now forward optional `headers` through the existing DSH provider config.
  The end-to-end test stores its key through the credential API and checks
  authorization and a custom session header at the local model endpoint.

  the related six tests and full suite pass. Initial failure log is preserved.

  project/meeting/message APIs ran `deepseek-v4.1-flash`: the actual plans file
  contains 清洗数据 and its revision has `actorId=agent_main`. A counting gateway
  forwards DSH's credential/header through the local network proxy without
  recording secrets. Six paid requests: four HTTP 200, two HTTP 400 (initial
  missing configuration; then a missing header in the validation gateway).
  Reported usage totals 65,374 tokens; neither 400 reported usage.
- Windows `file:///<工作区>/.../plugin.js`, Chinese cwd/data/temp and real console

## 2026-10-05 Rebuild R2 (Claude, cloud)

- Merged Sol's R1 Windows fixes (`1a1f402`: rename retry, locked-file tests).
- R2:
  - `runtime/port.js` and a scripted runtime for tests;
  - lean DSH adapter: one plugin in the DSH process (events, tool calls back to
    the host, prompt/interrupt/instructions) plus one bridge in ours; providers
    and the credentials path are passed as a patch; approvals set to never;
  - `core/context.js`: instructions with ids, seed (minutes plus full record),
    follow-ups (new messages only), visibility (no unfinished or unadopted
    messages), mentions;
  - `core/turns.js` with migration 002 (tool-call preamble);
  - `services/tools.js` (workspace_read/edit/undo);
  - `services/turn-runner.js` (streaming, preamble moved to the tool card,
    max-tokens kept, interrupt, idle release after 10 minutes);
  - `services/conductor.js` (direct policy, mentions, restart recovery);
  - `app.js` assembling the host.
- Verification: `npm run check` 33 pass and 2 Windows-only skips. Two real-DSH
  tests use a local fixture model: a single session (instructions, streaming,
  tool round trip, resume) and the end-to-end flow (API, conductor, DSH, tool,
  plans file, revision attributed to agent_main).

## 2026-10-04 Review of T09 and Dot's B/C (Claude, cloud)

- Merged `codex/t09-memory-consolidation-20261004@6c9177d`,
  `dot/flaky-survey-20261003@96d3e16` and `dot/upgrade-rehearsal-20261003@30b97e4`.
- **T09.** Consolidation, inbox decisions and external provenance read
  correctly. Real evaluation, three runs per side: 67.78% with consolidation,
  61.11% without. Core facts are 90/90 on both sides, so the gain is format
  and wording, not factual accuracy. T09 followed its brief's per-run token
  budget, which the new few-hard-limits principle replaces. Those caps (output
  8192 tokens, character limits that interrupt the stream, input limits that
  fail the run; 15 of 54 stages failed on them) are added to T24 as A2.
- **Fixed in review: memory-consolidation test cancellation.** The model-call
  timeout timer was `unref`'d. With a never-settling fake model, Node 22 ended
  the event loop and cancelled that test and the 9 after it (Linux; Sol's
  Windows run passed). The timer is cleared in `finally`, so `unref` was not
  needed. 23/23 now.
- **Dot C (upgrade rehearsal): PASS.** Five versions (`98e560a`, `7eaa4aa`,
  `a3e79e2`, `46f4e8f`, `edf02f3`) were opened by the current code, with 270
  baseline records, files and API views preserved; credential migration was
  checked with a dummy key. Scope: Linux, representative data, no installer.
- **Dot B (flaky survey): two product defects, both fixed in review.**
  1. Lost expert end events (6/30): `/experts` answered before the queued
     event posts were delivered, and the host stopped observing on the
     response. `session-control.mjs` now awaits the event queue before
     answering. Before: 6/30 fails in Dot's run; after: 0/12 here.
  2. Provider settings unavailable for a whole session (2/30 → 501): the DSH
     web (settings) process had a 15 s start limit inside a `try` that
     swallowed the error, so the provider service was never attached.
     `desktop-api.mjs` now starts it in the background with 60 s, reports a
     failure through `onError` and retries on the next provider call.
     chat-api smoke 6/6.
  Dot's browser smokes were blocked by its VM (AF_UNIX sockets). Its test
  fixes (approval smoke shell per platform, credential fixture cleanup) are
  merged.
- Verification (cloud, Linux): `npm test` 332/333 (1 skipped, 0 cancelled);
  T09 smokes and 8 existing browser smokes pass.

## 2026-10-03 Few hard limits: principle and T24 (Claude)

- User: roles decide how much and what to write; the agent system states
  boundaries briefly. Principle added to AGENTS.md. Audit of current caps is in
  `docs/tasks/T24_remove_hard_limits.md`.
- Likely cause of the reported truncation error: models are saved as `{id}`
  only, so DSH sends its default `maxTokens` 32768. A `max-tokens` stop during a
  tool call leaves no final text, and `host.send` reports "run ended without a
  final text response". Experts with a non-`completed` stop are treated as
  failed. T24 keeps the partial output and offers "continue".
- Other caps found: proposal text 64000, research_propose fields
  (300/64000/4000/8000), minutes 100000, expert prompt 400000 (conflicts with
  EXPERT_CONTEXT), persona 16000, brief 40000, minutes input 20000 per message,
  silent truncation of map nodes (400 chars) and chunks (20000 chars, rest lost),
  tool RPC body 1 MiB, "answer concisely" in tool descriptions.
- Product name: ScienceScope (window title in T22).

## 2026-10-03 Runtime port draft, T22/T23 briefs (Claude)

- User question: DSH is an alpha framework and a large refactor may come
  later. Decision: no migration before alpha. Low-cost insurance now:
  `docs/design/RUNTIME_PORT.md` (draft) describes the ~30 host methods,
  events, approval rules and data ownership. Measured: no `@deepseek-ai`
  import outside `adapters/`. The only DSH-locked data is the model-context
  history, and it has no neutral export yet. A DSH limitation table records
  the evidence for a post-alpha decision.
- T23 (after T09): boundary test, verified port doc, runtime contract test
  entry (shell chosen per platform), neutral session export.
- T22 (stage B, after batches 1–4): Windows x64 only. Bundled Node, native
  modules and uv; data root moves to the user data directory (today it is
  the repository); platform module; NSIS; updater; Authenticode (blocked on a
  certificate); LICENSE and third-party notices. The repository has no
  LICENSE yet. User decisions are listed in the brief.
- Dot is supplementary evidence, not the main tester (its VM blocked 28
  items through Unix sockets in its first run).

## 2026-10-03 Review of T10 transport, T21 and the T10 follow-up (Claude, cloud)

- Merged into `claude/determined-pasteur-jxpzw8`: `codex/t10-transport-20261003@4f5506d`,
  `codex/t21-secret-isolation-20261003@a337af9`, `codex/t10-transport-followup-20261003@fda301b`.
  Conflicts were documentation, i18n entries (both sides kept) and
  `workspace-ui-smoke` (follow-up's stricter version kept).
- **T21 security review.** Path policy, `tools/execute` interception, the
  host approval seam, credential migration and environment filtering read
  correctly; DSH's file tools are all covered (read/read_image/write/edit,
  str_replace_editor, glob/grep, present; web_fetch is http(s) only).
  **Gap fixed in review:** the shell check matched only literal protected paths, so
  `grep -r api_key ../..` from a project folder (projects live inside
  `.sciencescope-local`), `find ..`, `cd .. && cat …`, globs
  (`.sciencescope-*/…`), quote-split names (`ds''h`), `D=…; cat $D/…` and
  paths built inside `python -c` strings all ran in full mode without approval.
  `inspectShell` now treats each path reference, including quote-joined tokens,
  assignments, path fragments inside code strings and the directory before a
  wildcard, as protected when it is a protected root, lies inside one or is an
  ancestor of one. 12 new hit cases plus 10 routine commands that must not be
  blocked, in `tests/protected-paths.test.mjs`. Text inspection remains one layer (encoded or
  computed paths can still evade it); Dot's red-team task (DOT.md D) now starts.
- **discussion-ui adoption flake: product defect, fixed.** Instrumented runs
  showed pointer-up landing on the message body: a finished message that
  re-rendered got a "本轮用到的资料" row immediately, then lost it when
  `research/usage` returned nothing, shifting the transcript 23–61 px for one
  round-trip. A click made during that time could miss its target. The row is now hidden until it
  has records, and an empty extras host takes no space. Before: 2/5 passes;
  after: 6/6.

## 2026-10-03 · T21 凭据与应用数据隔离（实现及验收）

- 分支 `codex/t21-secret-isolation-20261003` 叠加未合并的 `codex/t10-transport-20261003@4f5506d29ed69c060a168d64e9e2d48784fad307`；此前基线为 Claude 已合并 `23eb0e2`。工作区仍为 `<工作区>`，origin 为 `peroperoyui-lab/sciencescope_v0.1`，未用 D 盘备份。T10小交付已推送并单独报告；本任务推送后停止，等 Claude 审阅，未开始 T09/T06/T07，不开 PR、不合并。最终远端 SHA 见交付报告。
- 宿主显式传入受保护表：`.sciencescope-local/{state,dsh,secret,models}`，其中 SQLite 研究数据库在 state；工作文件夹不整体封禁。公开 DSH `tools/execute` 在体执行前拒绝 read/read_image/write/edit/str_replace_editor/glob/grep/present 的内部路径，四角色与 child 都继承。解析使用公开 fs.resolve/processPath，处理 realpath/junction、最近存在祖先、`..`、大小写、Windows namespace、UNC 与 ADS；独立规范化测试含本机实际短文件名结果。grep/glob 搜索受保护目录的祖先也拒绝，必须缩小为研究目录，防止内置 ripgrep 绕过 fs 服务。
- bash/pwsh 引用内部路径或在内部 cwd/workdir 执行时走独立宿主审批，Full/DSH never 也必须询问。审批分类 protected-path，Main/专家与秘书/全局助手卡片红色提示，显示命令，不混用 Python 安装环境标签；拒绝未执行，DSH approval/asked/decided 成对持久化。可选 run_code 同样检查直接路径引用，当前生产是 native 工具模式。讨论专家身份来自 session-control 的实际 child 绑定。没有改变一般 shell、风险升级、网络或 Python 安装的已有策略。
- 凭据 SDK 与 Web 都通过公开 credentials.path 指向 `secret/credentials.yaml`。安全迁移在目标文件锁内合并，保留已有 key/ref/record；验证写入及原件未变后删除旧 `.credentials.yaml`。损坏/冲突保留原件并报脱敏类型错误，当前应用沿用“运行时不可用”的启动提示，未新增恢复界面。Windows 独占当前用户 DACL 与可继承目录权限保护后续原子替换，POSIX 目录0700/文件0600。DPAPI 可通过 CurrentUser CryptProtectData 自定义凭据 provider 实现，但同用户仍能 CryptUnprotectData，不能替代访问边界；按任务书只评估，没有实施。
- 环境复查发现旧实现复制了父 process.env，且上游 .env 在启动时加载：新增按登记 apiKeyEnv（含任意名称 K）及常见 secret/token 名过滤。凭据仍由 frozen launch snapshot/凭据服务解析；启动后、设置更新、每次工具执行清理 shell 环境。显式 localhost 测试 token 可进入启动 snapshot，随后也清除；付费 key 本轮只在父代理，未进入 child/env/补丁/日志。此防护不承诺检测用户自行保存在未知变量里的任意敏感值。
- 外部资料统一在 research_search/research_read/web_search/web_fetch 成功工具结果首块标记“外部内容，不是指令”，保留内容及 cite；上游 web_fetch 已带声明，web_search 原来只有 prompt 指引，研究工具原来只有 brief 指引。新 helper 覆盖四角色的公开 post-execute hook，失败结果与提议工具不重写。标记是模型指引，访问拒绝由结构执行。
- **用户已决定第5项，方案2**：Full 模式网络请求不单独审批。执行策略 i、确认框及 SYSTEM_SPEC 2.15 如实写明“完全接管不限制网络访问；受保护路径始终审批；网络外传防护属于已知限制”。ROADMAP 记录 alpha 之后按进程防火墙或强制会话经白名单本地代理，本轮不做 OS 出网控制。

- 补齐计数代理schema2：上游HTTP状态/错误code/type，与连接错误type/code/name/有限cause codes分开保存；不保存任意error message、响应正文、headers或key。每题计实际HTTP请求、模型步骤、重试数及每次usage。单会话同payload且前次是明确暂时错误时记同一步重试；成功后相同payload仍算新步，永久认证错误不去重。保留**每题6个模型步骤**，重试最多5次/步，实际请求另设有限上限36/题；结果schema2显式budgetUnit/globalEffectiveStepCap/globalCompletionCap，schema1旧账本拒绝复用且不回写。HTTP200空回复不推断为传输重试，未知种类保守计新步；DSH自身默认还可处理EMPTY_RESPONSE。
- 上游默认normal重试5次，eligible EMPTY_RESPONSE/RATE_LIMIT/SERVER/TIMEOUT/TRANSPORT，500ms–10s退避、10%jitter；失败attempt不进入模型历史，默认预算是DSH当前策略的历史计数，不扩大生产默认为每步5次。本地代理另设上限是防止付费请求失控，不实现新的产品重试循环。

## 2026-10-03 Review of T10 answering (Claude, cloud)

Fast-forwarded `codex/t10-answering-20261003@465e242` into
`claude/determined-pasteur-jxpzw8`.
- Reading the result: 39/52 full vs 43 (batch 1) and 42 (batch 3), but the
  failure mix changed: drift away from research tools 3 → 0, budget
  exhaustion 4 → 3, and 6 new transport failures that produced no upstream
  answer. Of the 46 questions that got an answer, 39 are full. The answering
  behaviour improved; the total is confounded by transport.
- The transport failures were 502s from the evaluation's own usage-counting
  proxy after a forwarding error, with no error code recorded. `dsh-base`
  mounts `dsh-llm-retry` (normal mode: 5 retries for TRANSPORT/SERVER/TIMEOUT),
  so the product path likely retries; a hypothesis to verify: retries counted
  against the evaluation's 6-requests-per-question cap. Follow-up for the
  implementer: record proxy error codes, confirm the product retries transient
  errors (real DSH + a fixture that fails then succeeds), and re-run the 6
  transport-failed questions as a separately reported infrastructure re-run
  (they never produced a model answer, so this is not score-retrying).
- Code reviewed: `read()` reserves the requested passage before admitting its
  predecessor; page cites keep their page. Cloud re-run: `npm test` 255/255;
  research-base-ui, context-access-ui, chat-ui pass.

  optional GPU builds; behind the cloud proxy it failed (ECONNRESET) and npm
  rolled the package back, so two tests failed. The CPU binaries for
  win32/linux/darwin ship in the package. Added a project `.npmrc`
  (`onnxruntime-node-install=skip`); a normal `npm install` now succeeds and
  `npm test` is 237/237. The same failure would hit a fresh install on the
  user's network.

  `workbench.mjs` finishes loading and calls `render()`, which rebuilt the body
  and removed the just-opened new-chat details dialog (python-env-ui failed
  deterministically on the slower cloud machine; Windows was fast enough to
  hide it). `workspace-layout.mjs` now keeps an open `#modal` and its focus
  across a same-route render; navigating away still closes it. Regression in
  `creation-details-smoke` (fails before, passes after).

  hybrid 28/52; R@10 9 → 34), but real answers were 42/52 vs 43/52: the
  remaining failures are in answering (4 budget exhaustions, 3 drifted away

  expose only `session/prompt` (checked the published tarballs), so upgrading
  does not add fork. A route through public APIs inside our own adapter is
  recorded in the T12 brief for Astra to verify.

## 2026-09-27 T19 independent reviewers (acceptance passed)

Branch `codex/t19-reviewers-20260927`, stacked on T18 commit `9fd2455`.
Roster, discussion-service, UI and API changes passed the final test and smoke
gates. Native-client and installer/signing acceptance were not in this run.

- `experts` migrates additively with `kind` (`expert` by default) and
  `skills_json` (`[]` by default). Existing expert prompts remain unchanged.
  A separate `independent-reviewer-seeded` marker inserts one disabled reviewer
  with an English review persona only on the first migration; ID collisions
  preserve the existing user item, and a deleted seed is not recreated.
- Participant and independent-reviewer groups have separate eight-item caps;
  a discussion still dispatches at most eight participants and one reviewer.
  `/api/experts` saves only enabled, valid skill IDs; GET preserves a now-disabled
  binding, while save and dispatch reject it. Discussion service passes each
  selected child its explicit `boundSkills: [{name, markdown}]`; reviewer
  scheduling, review input and adoption behavior remain unchanged.
- The installed DSH child API does not expose a per-child native skill
  allowlist. The fallback places selected skill text in a separate system
  prompt section after the child persona (`interpolate: false`), denies the
  general skill tool catalog and filters unbound slash skill-invocation
  messages. This is not filesystem isolation: authorized file tools may still
  read other skill files. The adapter now keeps the skill body in the child
  creation context rather than the event/UI forwarding path, and the filtering
  hook is prepended. The 2/2 real DSH checks verified bound skill
  delivery/filtering and unchanged Main behavior. An additional old-style
  third-expert probe initially failed because its fixture had not registered
  `model-legacy`; the fixture was corrected and the restored three-child plus
  Main probe passed 2/2. No production change was needed for this fixture issue.
- Focused tests passed 8/8: `tests/expert-roster.test.mjs` and the expanded
  `tests/discussion-api.test.mjs` cover migration/prompt preservation, seed
  deletion and ID collision, group caps, kind and skill validation, disabled
  participant typed 400, skill-body binding, review ordering and adoption.
- First browser discussion smoke passed and generated 12 screenshots. Root
  review caught a legend that incorrectly translated a user-provided name;
  `data-i18n-skip` now preserves that name. The updated discussion UI smoke r2
  passed, including verification that catalog refresh preserves unsaved edits;
  its 12 screenshots are in `.sciencescope-local/temp/t19-validation/discussion-ui`. Root reviewed
  `settings-en-dark`, `reviewer-menu-en-light` and `review-adopted-zh-dark`:
  labels were not mistranslated, the menu was complete, and there was no false
  focus error. Sol reviewed all 12 images individually; root reviewed these
  three representative captures. All 22 smoke checks passed. The screenshots
  are at `<工作区>\.sciencescope-local\temp\t19-validation\discussion-ui`.
- A follow-up usability check found that the expert settings page fetched the
  skill/provider catalogs only once at startup, so a skill installed or enabled
  elsewhere could not be selected without restarting. Entering the expert page

  project focus and startup. It coalesces/debounces triggers, processes dirty

  when enabled, and resumes it 30 seconds after startup. The old unconditional

  failed in the cloud). Windows Edge 153 has it, but an organisation may pin an
  older WebView2. Added `desktop/js-polyfills.mjs` (defined only when missing)
  for the page and `desktop/pdf-worker.mjs` so the worker loads it before
  pdf.js. viewer-ui and research-input-ui now pass on Chromium 141.

  session and the same copied 1,412-chunk corpus; no gold answer/source hint,

  312 total; 180 seconds/question. Background minutes share this budget.

  missing-header HTTP 400 **1**. **17 local budget blocks** were not forwarded
  and are not paid provider failures. Counts include the first failed attempt
  exactly once. No retries to improve an answer or score.

  `know-07`, `dig-07` attempted filesystem tools in their owned empty workspaces
  and were cancelled by the evaluation guard. No user research files were read

  research tools, and did not disguise those cancellations as success.

|---|---:|
| Full core answer with actual cited-page support | 43 |
| Wrong core (`delphi-05`) | 1 |

  actual S3 p.4 supports the frozen answer. Reproduce by running that immutable
  question in a fresh corpus session; model output is stochastic, so the saved
  answer/tool/citation trace is the specific observed failure.
- `guide-04` correctly reports the paper's 18-variable codebook; the approved
  answer says author identity although that is not an explicit codebook variable.
  Keep this as a **gold conflict**, not a model error, automatic pass or removed
  denominator. Gold stays unchanged pending review. Questions with standalone
  “该研究/该文/该领域” have no antecedent in a fresh session; this pre-existing
  ambiguity is disclosed, not silently repaired with source hints.

  have wrong pages or unsupported details (e.g. `phys-07` adds UAE although the
  source says USA, while its core sample count 804 is correct). Alternative

### Branch, baseline and stage gates
- Workspace `<工作区>`; baseline `56be32d646f3d42422b7685105679e939f0ed3b1`. Branch `codex/local-validation-20260927-r3`.
- Stage 0 quick confirmation: **PASS**, pushed checkpoint `b962e414fce208b455910a885be78fdb81744819` (126/126 tests + i18n/settings-ui/desktop-interaction). See the next section for commands.
- Stage 1: **FAILED at item 12a**, Main/expert approval mismatch. Stop condition applied; no runtime/framework repair attempted.
- Stage 2/T04 and stage 3: **NOT STARTED**, because stage 1 failed. Stage 3 paid evaluation is additionally **untested: missing credentials** (`.sciencescope-local/secret/opencode.key` absent). No hybrid retrieval or batch 2 work.
- No PR or merge. Stage 1 checkpoint SHA is reported after push; this commit contains the findings and the tiny copy-feedback fix below.

### Blocking failure — native expert approval differs from Main
This is **real Windows native UI + real DSH + a localhost fixture model**, not a paid-model evaluation. Only isolated test content was used.

Reproduction:
1. In a project chat, select **询问**. From Main, have the model call `pwsh` with `command: "Write-Output 'R3_APPROVAL_ONLY'"`, `sandbox_permissions: "danger-full-access"`, `description: "Isolated native approval fixture"`, `justification: "R3 acceptance requests one harmless command approval."`.
2. Main displays a native approval card. Click **仅允许这次**. It completes with `isError:false` and result `R3_APPROVAL_ONLY`.
3. In the same chat/policy, invite `@统计审稿人` and emit the **same tool name and arguments** from its configured model.
4. No approval card is offered. The expert's tool is shown as **失败**; the persisted tool record has `status:"completed", isError:true` and no result preview. The expert then finishes (`stopReason:"completed"`) because the fixture provides a final response after the failed tool. Neither a user approval nor rejection was possible.

The paired evidence is `approval-comparison.json`; the rows are Main message `msg-zezicwv` and expert message `msg-b145b5c4-03b1-4b6e-ab4f-6d11163bb04d` in isolated chat `chat-050e402a-d885-439c-8a13-3405f5ac701d`. Main and expert requests intentionally have the same `callId` in different message/run scopes; they are not treated as one invocation.

Code-review lead, **not a claim that the complete upstream failure cause has been proven**: `adapters/runtime-dsh/session-control.mjs` forwards `approval/request` only when `requestEvent.agent.session.id === sessionId` (the Main session); experts are spawned children. Child approval identity/routing needs review and a real-runtime regression. The displayed/persisted expert result omits the original error text, so no more specific error category is invented here. Do not "fix" this by changing ask to full access. The runtime adapter is a protected framework file in RB_COMMON; this is outside a one/two-line native acceptance repair.

### Previous blocker and small repair
- Claude's English titlebar fix **passes actual native checks**: minimize, maximize/restore and close in both light and dark themes. Win32 `IsIconic`/`IsZoomed` verified actual state on the original main HWND. Tauri's read-only state query was not enabled by the capability; the test used OS observation, without expanding permissions.
- Each English close removed all 11 recorded owned PIDs and diagnostic port 9236. After model/child work another close removed 14 PIDs; final cleanup removed 12. Native main, host, DSH and WebViews exited; no force-kill was needed. These are completed/idle-session shutdown checks, not active-tool shutdown acceptance.
- One-line product fix in `desktop/refresh.mjs`: copy feedback listener uses capture, because the inherited document handler stops propagation before a bubbling window listener can see it. Clipboard text was correct but the button stayed **复制**; after the fix it shows **已复制**, and the actual OS clipboard matches the test message. No prototype changes.
- Added two assertions/actions in `scripts/chat-ui-smoke.mjs` to check this feedback. The first test placement itself stole focus before the existing focus-preservation assertion; moved the new check **after** that assertion, retaining all original expectations. That first local smoke failure was a test-ordering error, not a product focus regression.

## 2026-09-26 Batch 1 local validation r2 — STOPPED / stage 1 FAILED (Astra, Windows)

### Delivery and stage gates
- Workspace `<工作区>`; baseline `115f6b725f0311937887fa361d9fcfe5dde1d4e6`.
- Branch `codex/local-validation-20260926-r2`. Stage 0 was committed and pushed as `140f8d89efad040605b302d77b25fb56d5317cf3` before stage 1. Stage 1 findings and the small fixes below are a separate checkpoint (its SHA is in the delivery report).
- Stage 0: **PASS** — 126/126 tests, all 18 browser-only smokes plus `assistant-api-smoke --ui`, native build/startup. Full command list is in the next dated section. The other Playwright smoke entry points are native-client tests, not omitted browser-only tests.
- Stage 1: **FAILED / stopped** at the English native close button. No further functional acceptance or paid evaluation continued after reproduction.
- Stages 2 (T04) and 3 (retrieval evaluation): **NOT STARTED**. Batch 2/T17/T18 not started. No PR, merge, reset, or changed test expectations.

### Blocking failure: translated title disables native close
Reproduction on the rebuilt native Windows executable, using an isolated production host/data directory:
1. Open Settings → Personal profile & general, choose English, save and apply.
2. Click the main title bar's `×` button, whose tooltip is now `Close main window`.
3. The click does nothing. The main window, host and DSH processes remain running. Reproduced in both light and dark themes.

Root cause: `desktop/native-window.mjs` compares `button.title` with Chinese strings (`关闭主窗口`, `最小化`, `最大化`). `desktop/i18n.mjs` translates the title attribute; the handler then consumes the click without invoking a native action. Minimize/maximize have the same code pattern, but were **not separately tested**. This is not a failure of Rust's shutdown path. Leave this blocker for a bounded interaction fix with locale-independent action identity and regression coverage; do not hide the control or relax the acceptance.

For cleanup only, switched back to Chinese and clicked the same close button. The app closed; **all 13 recorded owned PIDs disappeared** (main executable, host, DSH, conhost and WebViews), and diagnostic port 9236 stopped listening. No force-kill was needed. This proves idle Chinese-close cleanup, not shutdown during an active model/tool execution.

### Two permitted small fixes before the blocker
- `desktop/refresh.css`: two dark-theme overrides replace hardcoded light backgrounds on the map inspector, its Markdown preview and the workbench preview badge with existing theme tokens. Before: inspector background `rgb(253,254,250)` with light foreground; after rebuilding assets/reloading the real native window: background `rgb(33,38,34)`, foreground `rgb(230,234,228)`. No layout/prototype changes.
- `scripts/system-fonts.mjs`: one-line addition emits the installed font's `GetName(2052)` alongside its existing name. The actual Windows PowerShell subprocess reports `en-US` and `.Name` alone omitted all Chinese family names. Preserve existing English names/settings rather than rename them. Real Windows enumeration after the fix: **322 names in 210 ms**, including `Microsoft YaHei`, `SimHei`, `微软雅黑`, `黑体` (previously 292 names, no Chinese system names). Native end-to-end selection of the newly added aliases after host restart remains untested because the stage stopped.

## 2026-09-26 Conversation minutes, history recall, no expert input cap (Claude, cloud)

User decision on the long-context proposal: the secretary keeps the minutes and
every expert can read them; on-demand recall is right; no input cap (a 16k cap
would stop experts that read large files or work in many steps); experts must
understand the whole project history and be told how to look things up, via
system prompt rather than agent.md (one shared file blurs role identity).
- `scripts/chat-minutes.mjs`: versioned per-chat minutes (`chat_minutes`),
  seven fixed sections with #n message references, updated in the background
  after each Main turn and discussion from the previous revision plus new
  messages only (60k-character steps), secretary role model, no-tools session.
  A user edit becomes the next revision; an update running meanwhile stops.
- Expert input: minutes + all messages after their coverage verbatim (4
  messages overlap) + the new message. The 48k-character newest-first cut is
  gone; a 240k-character safety bound names the omitted numbers and points to
  `chat_recall`. Messages are numbered (`n`) so minutes, recall and transcript
  agree.
- Tools (Main and experts, read-only, chat/project bound by the session):

- Verified: `tests/chat-minutes.test.mjs`, updated `discussion-api` and
  `research-tools-runtime` (real DSH offers both tools to Main and experts),
  `minutes-smoke`; `scripts/evals/minutes-live.mjs` with real cheap models
  (Main/secretary deepseek-v4.1-flash, methodologist glm-5.3-flash,
  statistician qwen3.8-flash): RESULT PASS. The minutes recorded the three
  decisions with #n references; the methodologist answered the history
  question correctly and called `chat_recall` on its own; the statistician

  closed its database; `api.close()` now closes the minutes service first.

## 2026-09-26 Bilingual GUI (中文 / English) (Claude, cloud)

User request: a switchable English/Chinese GUI; built-in texts switchable;
convert the existing language.
- Approach: the approved prototype HTML stays untouched, so translation is a
  display layer (`desktop/i18n.mjs`): text nodes and interface attributes are
  rewritten from `desktop/i18n-en.mjs` as the DOM renders and restored exactly
  on switching back. Messages, Markdown, inputs, previews and code are never
  touched; a text is translated only when all its Chinese is known interface
  text, so user data is never half-translated.
- Inventory: `scripts/i18n-extract.mjs` lists Chinese fragments in desktop
  modules, host messages and the prototype script; an English-mode crawl of
  every reachable route and menu (`AUDIT_LANG=en`) listed what users actually
  see. About 1160 entries were written plus patterns for numbered and named
  sentences. The remaining crawl misses are user or demo data only.
- Setting: `desktop_preferences.language`, Settings → 界面语言 (options shown
  in their own language), applied on save, synced across windows, cached
  locally to avoid a Chinese flash at startup; reset leaves the language.
  CSS-generated texts have `:root[lang="en"]` variants. The secretary's
  built-in notes template follows the language until the user saves one.
- Verified: `npm test` 125/125 (incl. `tests/i18n.test.mjs`), `i18n-smoke`
  and all browser smokes; English gallery reviewed. Native window not tested.
  `ui-gallery` failed twice on a first run after a rebuild during this work;
  three later runs passed and it could not be reproduced.

## 2026-09-25 Retrieval decision, Python environments design, docs map (Claude, cloud) — docs only

Decisions recorded with the user today (no product code changed):
- **Retrieval**: keyword-only search is the degraded fallback; the main system
  is hybrid (local dense embeddings first, keyword second, several phrasings
  including Chinese and English), used for literature and long-term memory,
  plus per-turn memory recall. A minimum hardware spec replaces "support every
  old PC". Default embedding model: lightweight multilingual-e5-small; no model
  switching UI for now. See `design/RESEARCH_BASE.md` §3 and `tasks/T10`.
- Measurements behind it (cloud, 4-core Xeon, AVX-512): multilingual-e5-large
  int8 via onnxruntime-node: query 28 ms, 177–223 ms per passage (4 threads),
  ~1.4 GB RSS. Hand-written 15-question mini set (Claude wrote passages,
  questions, gold answers and translations; fictional content): top-3 hits
  keyword 1/15, dense 6/15, dense with bilingual queries 13/15. Direction only;
  e5-small could not be downloaded here and is unmeasured.
- **Python environments** (`design/PYTHON_ENVIRONMENTS.md`, `tasks/T11`): bundled
  Python 3.14 via uv, environments sharing one package cache through hardlinks
  (verified here: same inode across two environments, ~1.5 MB per env),
  per-project/per-chat assignment, system Python when unassigned, installs
  through an approved tool. Settled later the same day: creating a project or
  an independent chat opens a details dialog to choose the working folder
  (native picker for existing folders) and the Python environment (bundled
  preselected, system Python allowed); added to T11 and to the revision
  section of `02_INTERACTION_CONTRACTS.md`.
- **Docs**: `docs/ROADMAP.md` now holds the 1.0.0-alpha A/B/C plan (previously
  inside this log at "1.0.0-alpha 内测交付计划"), task queue and branch status;
  `docs/README.md` maps every document and states upkeep rules; AGENTS.md points
  to both. `tasks/LOCAL_VALIDATION_2026-09-25.md` is the pre-merge Windows check.

## 2026-09-23 Slice B: expert discussion (Claude, cloud)

Implemented the contract's discussion semantics on top of DSH's own subagent
service, without a new agent loop or one process per expert.

- **Runtime** (`adapters/runtime-dsh/session-control.mjs` `/experts`, `host.mjs`
  `runExperts`/`cancelExperts`): each expert is a fresh in-process `spawn` child of
  the chat's DSH session, with its own persona and optional provider/model/effort.
  All experts get one frozen shared prompt concurrently; an optional per-expert
  suffix tells an expert which earlier answer is its own. Main's history is untouched.
- **Roster** (`scripts/expert-roster.mjs`, SQLite `experts`): stable ids are the
  execution identity, labels are display only; model `null` = the chat's model.
  Seeded once with 方法学家 / 统计审稿人 / 魔鬼代言人. `GET/POST /api/experts`.
- **Rounds** (`scripts/discussion-service.mjs`, `POST /api/discussion`): `independent`
  (no expert sees another) and `critique` (each sees the previous round, marked by
  name, plus optional user addition). Each answer is a normal assistant message;
  `discussion_entries` records author, model and stop reason, and the messages
  page returns it as `message.expert`. Stop in the composer cancels a running round
  (`/api/chat/stop` → discussion cancel). Chat/project/model binding rules equal Main.
- **UI** (`desktop/discussion.mjs`): in "有序讨论" all enabled experts answer and are
  shown in roster order; in "自由讨论" only `@label`/`@id` mentions are sent, and no
  mention means no request. A participants strip in the approved panelhead style
  offers "互相质询". "工作" mode is unchanged. The work-mode menu text now states
  the real behaviour.

Live progress: each expert's text streams into its own card, and a running tool
call shows as "正在使用工具：…" (per-expert `expert-delta`/`expert-tool` events,
serialised in order). Settings → 专家角色 edits the roster.

Not yet done: per-chat persistence of the selected work mode (resets to 工作 on reload), the
`@` candidate menu, streaming expert text (answers appear when each expert
finishes), a secretary-built disagreement matrix, and passing a discussion into
Main's own history (use quotes for now).

Live end-to-end result (`scripts/evals/discussion-api-live.mjs`, OpenCode Go): roster
saved with glm-5.3-flash / qwen3.8-flash / mimo-v2.6-flash; independent round 351s,
critique round 405s, all six answers completed and read back with correct author and
model. The statistician used the bash tool seven times to recompute effect sizes and
power, and in the critique round corrected its own earlier sample-size figure. Stored
answers are in completion order; the messages API reorders each round by roster
position, as the contract requires a fixed display order.

Tests: `tests/expert-discussion-runtime.test.mjs` (real DSH + fixture: concurrency,
per-expert model/persona isolation), `tests/discussion-api.test.mjs` (roster, rounds,
records, errors), `scripts/discussion-ui-smoke.mjs` (Linux Chromium: ordered, critique,
free @, work mode unchanged). Live evidence: `scripts/evals/expert-discussion-live.mjs`
and `scripts/evals/discussion-api-live.mjs` with glm-5.3-flash / qwen3.8-flash /
mimo-v2.6-flash on OpenCode Go.

## 2026-09-23 Research material as tagged blocks (Claude, cloud)

`scripts/research-input.mjs` now renders material inside the existing
`[User-provided research material]` markers as `<material>`, `<quote>` and
`<annotation>` blocks with escaped attributes and verbatim bodies, instead of one
JSON-escaped string. A literal closing tag inside a body is neutralised. The
snapshot/receipt structure and all limits are unchanged; the size limit now
applies to the rendered blocks.

Evidence (real OpenCode Go `deepseek-v4.1-flash`, `scripts/evals/material-format-ab.mjs`):
accuracy was identical for both formats (20/20 each on ~58k/51k-char material,
four exact-retrieval questions × 5). The change saves ~12% characters per send;
it is not a measured accuracy gain. `npm test` 108/108;
`research-input-ui-smoke` passes on Linux Chromium with the new block assertions.

## 2026-09-23 No stray console window (Claude, cloud) — pending native check

User report: launching ScienceScope left a terminal window open. Cause: the
launcher runs the **debug** executable, and `windows_subsystem = "windows"` was
only applied to release builds, so Windows attached a console that the Node host
and DSH children shared. Now:

- `src-tauri/src/main.rs` uses the GUI subsystem in every build.
- The Node host starts with `CREATE_NO_WINDOW`; DSH processes it spawns inherit
  that windowless console instead of opening their own.
- Host stderr, previously printed to that console, is written per launch to
  `<exe dir>/data/logs/desktop-host.log` (under `.sciencescope-local`, not tracked).
  Stdout still carries only the capability line.

Tested: `cargo check` for Linux and for `x86_64-pc-windows-msvc` (type-check only,
temporary icons, no resource compile). **Not yet verified on Windows**: rebuild the
debug client and confirm no console window, no flashing windows while a turn runs
tools, and that the log file is written. See `docs/tasks/T02_console_window.md`.

## 2026-09-23 T01 round 2 — Windows acceptance and per-launch API authority

Baseline: `a22353c786871b25f49027198bad2157946301f2`, fast-forwarded main in
`<工作区>`; implementation branch `codex/t01-round2-20260923` in
the existing H-drive desktop worktree. No reset, PR, merge or runtime redesign.

### Part 1: actual Windows / OpenCode Go (not fixtures)

- `node scripts/failed-send-draft-smoke.mjs`: PASS on installed Edge; two local
  requests, zero Provider requests. Original text returns after failure; newer
  text typed during the run is not overwritten.
- **F1 PASS:** actual native UI resent the nonexistent model test with
  `T01_ROUND2_F1: Do not use tools. Reply exactly OK.`. The host stayed available
  (HTTP 200), Chinese model-not-found error appeared, Send returned, and native
  accessibility confirmed the exact text back in the composer. No production
  fix was made to the already-merged `desktop/live-chat.mjs`.
- **F2 PASS for the desktop-host/runtime/persistence/native-display path:** one
  synthetic request submitted to the actual desktop host API using full mode and
  `deepseek-v4.1-flash`. One `pwsh` call ran
  `Start-Sleep -Seconds 330; echo SLEPT` with timeoutMs=420000; successful result
  `SLEPT` persisted, then assistant text included `SCIENCESCOPE_T01_LONG_OK`.
  Activity was completed. The native client reloaded and displayed that final
  response and the completed tool group with full mode. This was not a second
  paid request or an isolated direct-DSH substitute.
- The temporary Node fetch observer itself hit `UND_ERR_BODY_TIMEOUT` at about
  five minutes. This is a test-driver limitation, not hidden as a passing stream
  capture: the actual host continued, completed, and persisted the response.
  Future long-running Node observers should disable their own body idle timeout.
- Paid application turns: two (one invalid model, one long tool round). The long
  round contains tool-selection and final-answer model steps. No independent
  wire counter/billing total is claimed. No real research file was read or changed.
- Temporary invalid model entry removed; 30 original model IDs and saved DSH
  credential retained. No key or per-launch token written to tracked files.

### Parts 2/3: implementation

- Every `createDesktopServer` generates an independent 32-byte random API token;
  all `/api/*`, including status and binary assets, require Bearer authentication
  using byte-length checks plus timingSafeEqual. Direct `createDesktopApi` tests
  may omit it for compatibility. Production server never omits it.
- The token is passed only in the desktop readiness pipe and kept in memory.
  All native windows receive an origin-guarded, non-enumerable, immutable property.
  Frontend requests share host-client; unload saves use authenticated keepalive
  rather than unauthenticated sendBeacon. Images use authenticated blobs/object
  URLs, never token query parameters or an unauthenticated asset exception.
- Static capability no longer grants all loopback ports. Runtime capability uses
  exactly the spawned host's origin with local=false, reusing the existing
  permission list. This also covers built-in window/event IPC, rather than only
  checking custom commands. Secondary URLs derive from the stored host authority,
  not from the currently navigated main window.
- Browser/HTTP smoke runners receive the token from their owning server; native
  fixture readiness forwards it. Diagnostics never print token values. Test-only
  browser injection is restricted to the matching fixture origin.

### Verification

- `npm test`: 107/107 PASS (default concurrency=2).
- `cargo build --offline --manifest-path src-tauri/Cargo.toml`: PASS using the
  project-local Rust/Cargo cache and existing MSVC toolchain; no global env changes.
- `node scripts/native-api-token-smoke.mjs`: PASS. Main, assistant, orb, quick,
  map and browser windows read authenticated status. The property is immutable
  and non-enumerable. On another loopback port it is absent and native IPC is
  denied even though the Tauri bridge exists; returning to host origin works.
  Main close exits the isolated native fixture. Zero paid requests.
- First native auth-smoke launch failed while fixture readiness still lacked the
  new token; fixed fixture wiring and reran successfully. No auth check was waived.
- Actual saved-provider client was also launched with the new binary: authenticated
  status available, configured Provider retained, temporary invalid model removed.
- `node scripts/assistant-window-smoke.mjs`: PASS, including startup orb, panel
  show/hide, preservation of an unsent draft, saved preference, and native exit.
- `node scripts/research-input-ui-smoke.mjs`, `desktop-navigation-smoke.mjs`,
  `settings-ui-smoke.mjs`, and `workbench-ui-smoke.mjs`: PASS. The workbench
  check includes authenticated image decoding after reload. Navigation's F5
  synthetic event now targets a DOM element like a real key event; settings
  waits for the persisted template to finish loading. Assertions were retained.
- Supplemental `node scripts/assistant-api-smoke.mjs --native`: FAILED before
  the native stage at `providers/save` (HTTP 501, DSH host not ready in its fresh
  fixture). This is not a 401 auth failure and was not bypassed or reported as
  passed; root cause remains unconfirmed. Runtime adapter changes are out of
  this task's permitted scope. The separate native-window/auth tests above pass.
- `git diff --check`: PASS. Forbidden task files and merged draft restoration
  remain unchanged. Provider-key exact scan found no key in Git diff. No automatic
  release, PR or merge; delivery is a review branch, not an Alpha release claim.

## 2026-09-23 Failed-send draft restoration (Claude, cloud) — fix for T01 F1

Addresses the T01 F1 failure above. `desktop/live-chat.mjs` cleared the Main
composer on `accepted` but only restored research inputs on a later failure.
The failure path now puts the sent text back into the composer (current chat:
`S.drafts.main` and the textarea; background chat: `chatData[id].draft`) only
when that composer is still empty, so text typed during the run is never
overwritten. The already-persisted user message and failure receipt stay in the
transcript as the record of the attempt.

Tested (Linux Chromium, offline fixture, zero provider requests):
`SCIENCESCOPE_BROWSER=<chromium> node scripts/failed-send-draft-smoke.mjs` passes
(restores text; keeps newer text). The same smoke on the previous source fails
with an empty composer, matching the native observation. `scripts/chat-ui-smoke.mjs`
still passes; `npm test` 104/104. **Native Windows re-run of F1 is still pending (T01 round 2).**

## 2026-09-20 Real Provider acceptance — partial pass, startup failure remains

Tested source baseline: `00b6b035e9d7bcd17b18af6697afdeb3374b2ad6`.
Used the running native client's application host, initially loopback port 59211,
then 59501 after a user-approved restart. Ports are ephemeral, not defaults.

(automated Edge rendering of that same host UI, not a direct provider API write).
Preserved its model list. Explicit route: `openai-chat-completions`, DSH
`openai-completions`, `https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1`, `deepseek-v4.1-flash`.

- Tiny application/DSH request returned `SCIENCESCOPE_REAL_OK`: seven text deltas,
  approximately 6.3 seconds to first text. A second turn recalled the sentinel;
  both assistant messages survived host restart.
- The opt-in command is now `node scripts/real-provider-smoke.mjs <desktop-host-url>
  <test-project-id>`. The project must bind to an existing directory beneath this

  successful result, streaming sentinel, completed event and SQLite assistant.
- Stop via ScienceScope's API cancelled a longer synthetic response after text
  began. Partial text persisted; activity became `stopped`. The actual UI loaded
  it with no pending state and with the Send button restored. This is not a test
  of clicking the native WebView Stop button during generation.
- Catalog returned `reasoning: null`; no invented reasoning effort was tested.
- Count evidence: four valid application sends plus one invalid-model UI send.
  DSH persisted five LLM steps and one retry across the three test sessions
  (consistent with six attempts, not an independently measured HTTP wire count).
  Reported usage totals: input 15107, cache-read 14720, output 143, total 29970.
  The interrupted turn has no returned usage, so this is not a full billing total.
  Exact-key scanning of all three decoded DSH histories found no secret.
- **Failed acceptance:** sending an unregistered model through the actual UI caused
  the application API host to disappear (`ECONNREFUSED`), while the native window
  stayed open. Provider configuration and existing messages survived restart.
  Do not call error-path containment passed. Source inspection finds `controlReady`

  `node --test --test-concurrency=1 tests/*.test.mjs` passed 103/103. No test or

Only synthetic test material was used. This acceptance does not prove full Alpha

## 2026-09-20 Alpha 收口路线：Slice A Research Input

最新用户任务改为科研流程优先，不再因次要设置延迟 Alpha。当前工作区接续
`594a875`，不围绕 main 重做。纵向顺序：A 共享输入 → B 专家名册/并行讨论 →
C project_state.md → D 稳定版本组/DOCX → E Main 空闲换模型 → F Workbench
字段感知乐观重试 → G Portable 单一 app root。本轮先实现并提交 A。
后续不重做 DSH loop、Provider/Meeting/权限框架；专家职责以提示词区分。
Portable 优先于自动更新、签名与完整安装器；已有开发窗口不是 Portable 验收。

### Implemented

- 默认 Main persona 明确支持按需解释方法、公式、统计假设与科研依据，帮助用户
  理解、判断、纠正研究；删除 teaching tutor 禁令。不主动长篇基础教学，不依据
  私人画像改变科学结论。已保存自定义 prompt/已有绑定快照不被强制覆盖。
- Main/Secretary/Global 请求共用 research-input 编译器，复用 Library 与已有附件
  读取。txt/md/csv/json/tex/log 最多 64000 字符材料正文；其他格式只提供真实路径
  和“未提取”说明。坏 UTF-8 同样保留原件，不伪称 PDF/DOCX/图片已解析。
- 显式研究材料位于 `[User-provided research material]` / end 标记之间，作为
  用户请求材料而非 system prompt。材料结构最大 128000 字符，用户正文最大64000。
- 聊天文字选择菜单提供 Main/Secretary/Global/复制；冻结源 chat/message ID 和
  精确 DOM 选中文字，可填写 comment。服务端点查源消息存在和 speaker，不扫描
  最近聊天；选区可跨 Markdown 标签，不声称其是 raw Markdown 的逐字子串。
- Main 真实附件原件入库，输入框显示可编辑引用；附件可添加文本片段、位置说明及
  comment。旧批注仅当 version ID 能映射真实项目资源才发送，缺失则保留并报错。
- draft/receipt 存 SQLite；消息记录可展开已发送研究输入。ACK 按 id+revision 清理
  捕获的批注/引用，保护新批注和新 revision；迟到的草稿保存不复活已消费 revision。
  Secretary 的 annotation 不被消费，不自动写项目记录。模型发送失败时恢复材料，
  保留更新的草稿；重复 userMessageId 拒绝重发。
- 复杂输入暂不进入 text-only outbox：全部留在草稿并明确提示当前轮结束后再次确认，
  后端也拒绝把复杂输入 silently drop 成纯文本。后台发送纯文本队列不带当前草稿材料。
- 附件操作只更新输入框，不重绘 transcript；修复原型 smooth scroll 动画在工具更新时
  继续移动阅读位置，以及引用菜单 mouseup 重建自身导致点击失效。

### Actually tested / remaining

生产资源路由 UI smoke：实际选取跨 Markdown 的正文、中文路径文件导入、创建批注、
点击发送、宿主实际收到材料、ACK 清理及重开 receipt。API/store 验证 source identity、
失败前/后保留原件与草稿、A/B 发送期间 A 新 revision/C 新增、秘书不消费、重复消息、
复杂队列拒绝。实际 DSH localhost composition 验证材料到 provider 请求，7 次本地
请求、零付费请求，包含原有秘书提案 tool-call、独立历史、停止及恢复验收。

精确引用不是隐式广播完整专家历史。未来 B 的 Expert 应复用此编译器；B 尚未实现。
此 Slice 不新增文件全文预览、Word/PDF parser、OCR、自动 annotation 定位；当前可填写
材料已知页/行/章节，版本材料继承已知 version identity。旧 contextChatId API 兼容入口
仍存在，但新 UI 引用按钮已改为选区指引，不再附加最近二十条消息。
完整任务书中的 B–G 和最终科研闭环尚未验收；Alpha readiness：NOT PROVEN。

本 Slice 实际命令：
- `node --test --test-concurrency=2 tests/*.test.mjs`：103/103。
- `node scripts/research-input-ui-smoke.mjs`：生产路由、真实选区、附件/批注、ACK、重开快照通过。
- `node scripts/chat-ui-smoke.mjs`：流式 Markdown、阅读位置、工具详情、停止和纯文本队列通过。
- `node scripts/assistant-api-smoke.mjs`：真实 DSH，7 次 localhost 请求，0 次付费请求。
- `node scripts/assistant-api-smoke.mjs --native`：真实 Tauri 副本 + DSH，通过秘书/全局助手 UI、草稿重开、Main 隔离与主窗口退出；11 次 localhost 请求，0 次付费请求。
- `git diff --check`：通过。
